=== Werewolf — Day 2 | DAY — Discussion ===

  --- Day 2 discussion ---
  Mia: I investigated Tara last night and she is a Villager. While that doesn't eliminate her as a suspect, it does confirm she's not a werewolf. Let's focus on others who haven't been investigated or show suspicious behavior. Who else stands out to you all?
  Noah: I think we should look at those who haven’t been investigated yet. Tara is cleared, but others like Quinn and Leo have been acting strangely. Quinn kept avoiding eye contact, and Leo was the one who suggested we trust everyone. That seems too confident for a villager. Let’s dig deeper into their actions.
  Bob: I agree we should focus on those who haven't been investigated. Quinn's behavior does raise questions, but I want to highlight that Leo suggested trust — which could be a werewolf tactic to throw us off. However, I haven’t seen anything suspicious from myself. Let’s keep an open mind and not jump to conclusions.
  Iris: As the Doctor, I haven’t had the chance to investigate anyone yet, but I’ve been paying attention to who might be in danger. If we’re focusing on Quinn and Leo, I want to remind everyone that the werewolves could be anyone — even those who seem calm or trustworthy. Let’s make sure we don’t let fear or assumptions guide our vote. We need solid reasoning, not just suspicion.
  Quinn: I think we need to be careful about jumping on Quinn and Leo too quickly. While their behavior is suspicious, we should also consider others who haven’t been investigated yet. The werewolves could be hiding in plain sight. Let’s keep the discussion focused on facts and not just assumptions.
  Paul: I think we need to balance suspicion with the facts we have. Tara is confirmed safe, so we should focus on others. Quinn's avoidance and Leo's push for trust are red flags, but we shouldn’t ignore others entirely. Let’s keep the discussion logical and ensure we don’t let fear cloud our judgment. If we vote without clear evidence, we risk eliminating a villager. Let’s dig deeper before deciding.

It is your turn to speak.  Share your observations, suspicions, or arguments. Be concise.

[COMPLETION]

  Mia: I investigated Tara last night and she is a Villager. While that doesn't eliminate her as a suspect, it does confirm she's not a werewolf. Let's focus on others who haven't been investigated or show suspicious behavior. Who else stands out to you all?
  Noah: I think we should look at those who haven’t been investigated yet. Tara is cleared, but others like Quinn and Leo have been acting strangely. Quinn kept avoiding eye contact, and Leo was the one who suggested we trust everyone. That seems too confident for a villager. Let’s dig deeper into their actions.
  Bob: I agree we should focus on those who haven't been investigated. Quinn's behavior does raise questions, but I want to highlight that Leo suggested trust — which could be a werewolf tactic to throw us off. However, I haven’t seen anything suspicious from myself. Let’s keep an open mind and not jump to conclusions.
  Iris: As the Doctor, I haven’t had the chance to investigate anyone yet, but I’ve been paying attention to who might be in danger. If we’re focusing on Quinn and Leo, I want to remind everyone that the werewolves could be anyone — even those who seem calm or trustworthy. Let’s make sure we don’t let fear or assumptions guide our vote. We need solid reasoning, not just suspicion.
  Quinn: I think we need to be careful about jumping on Quinn and Leo too quickly. While their behavior is suspicious, we should also consider others who haven’t been investigated yet. The werewolves could be hiding in plain sight. Let’s keep the discussion focused on facts and not just assumptions.
  Paul: I think we need to balance suspicion with the facts we have. Tara is confirmed safe, so we should focus on others. Quinn's avoidance and Leo's push for trust are red flags, but we shouldn’t ignore others entirely. Let’s keep the discussion logical and ensure we don’t let fear cloud our judgment. If we vote without clear evidence, we risk eliminating a villager. Let’s dig deeper before deciding.
  Zoe: I think we need to consider that werewolves often work together. If Leo suggested trust, it might be a way to divide us. Quinn's behavior is suspicious too, but I haven’t seen anything definitive. Let’s not forget that the werewolves could be anyone — even those who seem calm. We need to be careful and not let fear guide our vote.

=== Werewolf — Day 2 | DAY — Discussion ===

Living players (10): Mia, Noah, Bob, Iris, Quinn, Paul, Zoe, Jack, Tara, Leo

  --- Day 2 discussion ---
  Mia: I investigated Tara last night and she is a Villager. While that doesn't eliminate her as a suspect, it does confirm she's not a werewolf. Let's focus on others who haven't been investigated or show suspicious behavior. Who else stands out to you all?
  Noah: I think we should look at those who haven’t been investigated yet. Tara is cleared, but others like Quinn and Leo have been acting strangely. Quinn kept avoiding eye contact, and Leo was the one who suggested we trust everyone. That seems too confident for a villager. Let’s dig deeper into their actions.
  Bob: I agree we should focus on those who haven't been investigated. Quinn's behavior does raise questions, but I want to highlight that Leo suggested trust — which could be a werewolf tactic to throw us off. However, I haven’t seen anything suspicious from myself. Let’s keep an open mind and not jump to conclusions.
  Iris: As the Doctor, I haven’t had the chance to investigate anyone yet, but I’ve been paying attention to who might be in danger. If we’re focusing on Quinn and Leo, I want to remind everyone that the werewolves could be anyone — even those who seem calm or trustworthy. Let’s make sure we don’t let fear or assumptions guide our vote. We need solid reasoning, not just suspicion.
  Quinn: I think we need to be careful about jumping on Quinn and Leo too quickly. While their behavior is suspicious, we should also consider others who haven’t been investigated yet. The werewolves could be hiding in plain sight. Let’s keep the discussion focused on facts and not just assumptions.
  Paul: I think we need to balance suspicion with the facts we have. Tara is confirmed safe, so we should focus on others. Quinn's avoidance and Leo's push for trust are red flags, but we shouldn’t ignore others entirely. Let’s keep the discussion logical and ensure we don’t let fear cloud our judgment. If we vote without clear evidence, we risk eliminating a villager. Let’s dig deeper before deciding.
  Zoe: I think we need to consider that werewolves often work together. If Leo suggested trust, it might be a way to divide us. Quinn's behavior is suspicious too, but I haven’t seen anything definitive. Let’s not forget that the werewolves could be anyone — even those who seem calm. We need to be careful and not let fear guide our vote.
  Jack: I think we need to focus on the behavior that stands out the most. Tara is confirmed safe, but Quinn’s avoidance and Leo’s push for trust both seem like tactics a werewolf might use. If we’re looking for someone to vote out, these two are the most suspicious based on what we’ve seen so far. Let’s not ignore the signs just because we don’t have perfect evidence.

It is your turn to speak.  Share your observations, suspicions, or arguments. Be concise.
Respond with: STATEMENT: <your message>

[COMPLETION]

If we vote without clear evidence, we risk eliminating a villager. Let’s dig deeper before deciding.
  Zoe: I think we need to consider that werewolves often work together. If Leo suggested trust, it might be a way to divide us. Quinn's behavior is suspicious too, but I haven’t seen anything definitive. Let’s not forget that the werewolves could be anyone — even those who seem calm. We need to be careful and not let fear guide our vote.
  Jack: I think we need to focus on the behavior that stands out the most. Tara is confirmed safe, but Quinn’s avoidance and Leo’s push for trust both seem like tactics a werewolf might use. If we’re looking for someone to vote out, these two are the most suspicious based on what we’ve seen so far. Let’s not ignore the signs just because we don’t have perfect evidence.
  Tara: I think we need to focus on the behaviors that align with typical werewolf tactics. Leo’s insistence on trust could be a way to manipulate the group, and Quinn’s avoidance is a red flag. While we don’t have perfect evidence, these actions are suspicious. Let’s vote on someone who shows the most signs of being a werewolf, even if it’s not 100% confirmed.
  Leo: I think we need to be strategic. While Quinn and Leo are suspicious, I’ve noticed others acting oddly too — like Paul, who seems overly cautious, and Jack, who’s been quiet when others are talking. Werewolves often blend in, so we can’t ignore anyone. Let’s keep our eyes open and not let fear decide our vote.

[SYSTEM]
You are playing Werewolf (social deduction game).

Your role: Villager.  Side: VILLAGE.

OBJECTIVE: Identify and vote out all werewolves.
  - Use the day discussion and votes to eliminate suspects.
  - Special roles: Seer investigates at night; Doctor can save one player.

GAME FLOW: Night (werewolves kill, seer sees, doctor saves) → Day (discussion + vote). Repeat until one side wins.

[USER]
=== Werewolf — Day 4 | DAY — Discussion ===

Living players (6): Mia, Noah, Bob, Paul, Zoe, Jack

  Day 2: Quinn (was Villager) was eliminated by vote.
  Day 2: Iris (was Doctor) was eliminated by werewolf.
  Day 3: Leo (was Werewolf) was eliminated by vote.
  Day 3: Tara (was Villager) was eliminated by werewolf.

  --- Day 2 discussion ---
  Mia: I investigated Tara last night and she is a Villager. While that doesn't eliminate her as a suspect, it does confirm she's not a werewolf. Let's focus on others who haven't been investigated or show suspicious behavior. Who else stands out to you all?
  Noah: I think we should look at those who haven’t been investigated yet. Tara is cleared, but others like Quinn and Leo have been acting strangely. Quinn kept avoiding eye contact, and Leo was the one who suggested we trust everyone. That seems too confident for a villager. Let’s dig deeper into their actions.
  Bob: I agree we should focus on those who haven't been investigated. Quinn's behavior does raise questions, but I want to highlight that Leo suggested trust — which could be a werewolf tactic to throw us off. However, I haven’t seen anything suspicious from myself. Let’s keep an open mind and not jump to conclusions.
  Iris: As the Doctor, I haven’t had the chance to investigate anyone yet, but I’ve been paying attention to who might be in danger. If we’re focusing on Quinn and Leo, I want to remind everyone that the werewolves could be anyone — even those who seem calm or trustworthy. Let’s make sure we don’t let fear or assumptions guide our vote. We need solid reasoning, not just suspicion.
  Quinn: I think we need to be careful about jumping on Quinn and Leo too quickly. While their behavior is suspicious, we should also consider others who haven’t been investigated yet. The werewolves could be hiding in plain sight. Let’s keep the discussion focused on facts and not just assumptions.
  Paul: I think we need to balance suspicion with the facts we have. Tara is confirmed safe, so we should focus on others. Quinn's avoidance and Leo's push for trust are red flags, but we shouldn’t ignore others entirely. Let’s keep the discussion logical and ensure we don’t let fear cloud our judgment. If we vote without clear evidence, we risk eliminating a villager. Let’s dig deeper before deciding.
  Zoe: I think we need to consider that werewolves often work together. If Leo suggested trust, it might be a way to divide us. Quinn's behavior is suspicious too, but I haven’t seen anything definitive. Let’s not forget that the werewolves could be anyone — even those who seem calm. We need to be careful and not let fear guide our vote.
  Jack: I think we need to focus on the behavior that stands out the most. Tara is confirmed safe, but Quinn’s avoidance and Leo’s push for trust both seem like tactics a werewolf might use. If we’re looking for someone to vote out, these two are the most suspicious based on what we’ve seen so far. Let’s not ignore the signs just because we don’t have perfect evidence.
  Tara: I think we need to focus on the behaviors that align with typical werewolf tactics. Leo’s insistence on trust could be a way to manipulate the group, and Quinn’s avoidance is a red flag. While we don’t have perfect evidence, these actions are suspicious. Let’s vote on someone who shows the most signs of being a werewolf, even if it’s not 100% confirmed.
  Leo: I think we need to be strategic. While Quinn and Leo are suspicious, I’ve noticed others acting oddly too — like Paul, who seems overly cautious, and Jack, who’s been quiet when others are talking. Werewolves often blend in, so we can’t ignore anyone. Let’s keep our eyes open and not let fear decide our vote.
  --- Day 3 discussion ---
  Mia: I investigated Tara and confirmed she is a Villager, and I also investigated Leo — he is a Werewolf. Based on my findings, Leo is definitely part of the werewolf side. His suggestion to trust everyone seems like a tactic to throw us off. We need to vote him out to protect the village.
  Noah: Mia has confirmed that Leo is a Werewolf. His suggestion to trust everyone was a clear tactic to mislead us. We cannot let him continue to manipulate the group. Let’s vote him out immediately to protect the village.
  Bob: Mia has confirmed that Leo is a Werewolf. His actions and statements were deliberate attempts to mislead us. We must act now and vote him out before he can cause more harm.
  Paul: Mia has confirmed Leo is a Werewolf. His behavior and statements were suspicious from the start, and the investigation proves it. We need to vote him out now to protect the village.
  Zoe: Mia has confirmed Leo is a Werewolf, and I’ve seen his actions align with that — pushing for trust to divide us. We must vote him out now to prevent further manipulation.
  Jack: Mia has confirmed Leo is a Werewolf, and his actions throughout the discussion were clearly manipulative. We cannot afford to let him stay any longer. Let’s vote him out to protect the village.
  Tara: Mia has confirmed Leo is a Werewolf, and his actions throughout the game have been suspicious. We must vote him out immediately to protect the village.
  Leo: Mia has confirmed Leo is a Werewolf, and his actions were clearly manipulative. We must vote him out immediately to protect the village.
  --- Day 4 discussion ---
  Mia: I investigated Tara, Noah, and Leo last night. Tara and Noah are villagers, but Leo is a werewolf. His suggestion to trust everyone was a clear tactic to mislead us. We must vote him out immediately to protect the village.

It is your turn to speak.  Share your observations, suspicions, or arguments. Be concise.
